Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. **RSFT r1 (seed=42) eval results:**
- rel_f1 = 0.4449
- ign_f1 = 0.4449
- evi_f1 = 0.8187
- EDCR = 0.6565
- Precision = 0.4607, Recall = 0.4302
- n_total = 985, format_compliance = 100%


- n_truncated = 213/985, n_predictions = 15307
- inference_time = 6033.4s (~100.6 min)
- n_field_complete = 770/985 (78.2%)

The model is Qwen3-4B with SFT adapter at seed 42, no quantization. Looking at the cross-seed comparison with the same config (3 epochs, alpha=128, lr=2e-5), seed 44 shows slightly better rel_f1 at 0.4655 compared to seed 42's 0.4449.

Both seeds pass the MVP-2 threshold, though seed 43 eval failed and needs a retry. The performance gap between seeds is modest—about 2 percentage points on rel_f1 and 1 point on evi_f1, which is expected variance for this task. I'm noticing a couple of issues: the registry shows mvp2_eval_rsft_r1 as RUNNING but the process is actually dead with results already saved, and cuda:2 has been sitting idle since the r1 eval finished earlier this evening.

The retry for seed 43 round 2 is allocated but hasn't been submitted yet, so it's not utilizing that available GPU. Looking at the field compliance, only about 78% of predictions have complete evidence fields—that 22% gap is concerning and wasn't flagged in the seed 44 comparison. Truncation is also hitting 21.6%, which matters for a document-level task. The fact that ign_f1 matches rel_f1 exactly makes sense for the re-annotated dev set since there are no ignored triples being filtered out.

Let me check on the r2a and r2b eval runs since they should still be progressing, and I want to cross-verify seed 44's metrics (rel_f1=0.4655, evi_f1=0.8087) against what we're seeing here. I should pull together the overdue r1 audit report with cross-seed comparison and the seed 43 diagnostic alert about the idle GPU, then track r2a/r2b status.

1. **r1 eval (seed=42)**: COMPLETED. Results: rel_f1=0.4449, evi_f1=0.8187. Process dead, registry still shows RUNNING. Inference time: 6033.4s (~100 min). **Passes MVP-2**.

2. **s43 eval**: FAILED (EXIT_CODE=2, wrong CLI args). Worker diagnosed and created `mvp2_eval_rsft_s43_r2` in ALLOCATED status. Not yet submitted.

3. **r2a eval**: Still running on cuda:1, at 86% (107/124 batches). 

4. **r2b eval**: Still running on cuda:3, at 88% (109/124 batches). Both r2a and r2b started within a minute of each other around 18:03-18:04 server time.

5. **CED reranker**: Still running on cuda:0.

6. **cuda:2 is idle**: s43_r2 is ALLOCATED but waiting to be submitted.

Looking at the audit reports now. For r1 eval, the metrics look solid—rel_f1 and evi_f1 both pass MVP-2 thresholds. There's a small cross-seed variance between s42 and s44, with s43 still pending. The truncation rate sits at 21.6% and field compliance at 78.2%, which means about 215 predictions are missing valid evidence. The EDCR metric shows 65.6% of citations are distractors, which is notable. The registry still shows RUNNING status even though the eval finished, so that's stale data.

For s43, the eval failed because the worker was using incorrect argument names—passing --data_dir instead of --data_path, missing --model_path entirely, and trying to use an invalid --gpu flag.  The worker caught this and created a retry, but s43_r2 is still sitting in ALLOCATED status while cuda:2 remains idle, which means it hasn't been submitted yet. That's wasting GPU resources that could be processing it.

I should also check on the r2a/r2b eval progress since they're at 86-88% completion and might finish within the next 10-15 minutes. And I want to pull the s44 results for a proper cross-seed comparison against s42.

Looking at the metrics across seeds, s42 and s44 show an interesting pattern: s44 has better relation extraction performance but slightly worse evidence F1 and EDCR scores, suggesting there's a trade-off in how different seeds balance these objectives. Now I'm checking the GPU allocation status for the r2a and r2b evaluations. The GPU allocations look correct across the whitelist, and both r2a and r2b loaded their respective LoRA adapters properly. The generation flags warning about temperature and top_p parameters is expected for eval runs since greedy decoding doesn't use those anyway. I'm ready to post the reports now.

exp_id: mvp2_grpo_ced_r2\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: KL explosion + OOM at step 18, num_iterations=2 confirmed as root cause\\n  depends_on: []\\n- id: D008\\n  date: 2026-05-14 12:07 UTC\\n  summary: D007 证伪：KL 爆炸根因是 kl_coef + QLoRA 4-bit 不兼容，非 num_iterations。全部 GRPO 重跑\\n    kl_coef=0\\n  rationale: 3 个 GRPO 实验（num_iterations=1）均在 step 5 触发 KL 爆炸早停（KL 达 10^10 量级）。对比 r1（kl_coef=0/未设）跑了\\n    80+ steps 才因 reward imbalance collapse。证据链：所有设 kl_coef=0.05 的实验全部 step 5 爆炸，未设的\\n    r1 稳定运行。机理：QLoRA 4-bit 量化噪声导致 ref model 和 training model 的 log-probs 存在系统性偏差，KL(π_θ||π_ref)\\n    从第一步就是天文数字。修复：kl_coef=0，依赖 reward weighting + save_steps + KL 监控（logging only）控制稳定性。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# 文献调研：GRPO/RLHF 训练中的\\\"先遗忘后恢复\\\"现象\\n\\n日期：2026-05-14\\n\\n## 背景\\n\\nQwen3-4B + GRPO + QLoRA 4-bit 训练文档级关系抽取，观察到：\\n- Reward 上升但 eval rel_F1 崩塌（ckpt-20: 0.0077 vs SFT baseline 0.406）\\n- lr=5e-5 和 lr=5e-6 下都出现\\n- 存在\\\"先遗忘后缓慢恢复\\\"的 U 型曲线（step-10=0.003 → step-90=0.211），但恢复不足\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 论文 1：Mitigating the Alignment Tax of RLHF\\n\\n- **作者**：Yifei Lin, Yuxin Tan, et al.\\n- **年份/Venue**：2024, EMNLP 2024\\n- **arXiv**：2309.06256\\n\\n### 核心发现\\n- 定义了\\\"alignment tax\\\"：RLHF 对齐后模型遗忘预训练/SFT 阶段习得能力的现象\\n- 发现简单的**模型参数平均**（插值 SFT 和 RLHF 后模型权重）在 alignment-forgetting Pareto 前沿上表现最优\\n- 提出 **Heterogeneous Model Averaging (HMA)**：对不同层使用不同插值比例\\n\\n### 与我们场景的相关性\\n- **直接相关**。GRPO 阶段的 rel_F1 崩塌是 alignment tax 的典型表现\\n- HMA 的层级插值思路可直接应用于 QLoRA adapter 权重（但 GRPO 路线已判死，仅留作参考）\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 论文 2：Online Merging Optimizers for Boosting Rewards and Mitigating Tax in Alignment\\n\\n- **作者**：Lu et al. (Qwen 团队)\\n- **年份/Venue**：2024, arXiv 2405.17931\\n- **开源**：github.com/QwenLM/online_merging_optimizers\\n\\n### 核心发现\\n- 在 RLHF 每个优化步骤中实时合并 RL policy 和 SFT 模型参数\\n- 在 Qwen 和 LLaMA 系列上验证有效\\n\\n### 与我们场景的相关性\\n- 高度相关但 GRPO 路线已判死，仅留作参考\\n\\n---\\n\\n## D031 Pivot: GRPO+QLoRA → RSFT + CED (2026-05-15)\\n\\n### 决策\\nGRPO+QLoRA 方向判死，pivot 到 Rejection Sampling Fine-Tuning (RSFT) + CED scoring。\\n\\n### 核心证据\\n- lr=5e-5 (ced_kl001_r4): best eval rel_f1=0.211 (step-90)，仅 SFT baseline (0.406) 的 52%\\n- lr=5e-6 (lr5e6_r1): 完整轨迹 ckpt-20→100 rel_f1 = 0.008/0.007/0.009/0.012/0.014 — 零恢复\\n- 所有 3 种 reward type (CED/extract-only/flat-NLI) 在 GRPO+QLoRA 下均 collapse\\n\\n### RSFT 技术约束\\n- **--quantize (4-bit) + merge_and_unload 在 RTX 5090 上有 CUBLAS 错误**，RSFT 训练不要用 quantize。bf16 Qwen3-4B 仅 ~8GB，无需量化。\\n- 运行目录必须是 `/root/autodl-tmp/`（freige 不是 pip install 的包，cd 到 freige/ 子目录会导致 import 找到内层空包）\\n\\n---\\n\\n## D038 重大修正：RSFT r1 实际成功 (2026-05-15)\\n\\n### 事件\\nD037 报告 RSFT r1 \\\"灾难性失败\\\"（rel_f1=0.0483），但这是 **TaskEvalCallback bug**——`--eval_max_docs 50` 评估 50 个 doc 的预测，却用全部 12275 个 gold triples 计算 F1，严重低估 rel_f1。\\n\\n### 真实结果\\ns44 全量 dev eval (985 docs): **rel_f1=0.4655 (+5.98pp), evi_f1=0.8087 (+1.08pp)** → MVP-2 PASS\\n\\n### TaskEvalCallback bug fix\\n已部署到 `rsft_trainer.py:118-120`。gold triples 现在过滤到 eval_max_docs 对应的文档子集。\\n\\n### 教训\\n- 在 callback 中用 subset eval 时，必须确保 gold 和 pred 在同一文档集合上计算\\n- 基于 callback 数字的任何结论都需要全量 eval 交叉验证\\n\\n---\\n\\n## ~~RSFT r1 灾难性失败根因~~ [D038 撤回]\\n\\n**以下分析基于 D037 的错误 rel_f1=0.0483。D038 确认该数字是 eval bug，实际 rel_f1=0.4655。**\\n\\n原分析中关于\\\"关系本体崩塌\\\"的观察可能来自 eval_max_docs=50 下的极小样本偏差，不代表模型真实行为。r1/s43/s44 的全量 eval 正在进行中，将给出真实 ablation 数据。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## RSFT r2a/r2b ablation [全量 eval 进行中] (2026-05-15)\\n\\n### 实验配置\\n\\n| 参数 | r1 (原始) | r2a (data fix only) | r2b (data + train fix) |\\n|------|----------|---------------------|------------------------|\\n| 数据 | 2718 samples | clean 2548 samples | clean 2548 samples |\\n| Epochs | 3 | 1 | 1 |\\n| LoRA alpha/r | 128/64 (scaling=2.0) | 128/64 (scaling=2.0) | 32/64 (scaling=0.5) |\\n| Learning rate | 2e-5 | 2e-5 | 1e-5 |\\n\\n### TaskEvalCallback 数字 [已知 buggy，仅供参考]\\n- r2a: rel_f1=0.0509 (buggy), eval_loss=0.7547\\n- r2b: rel_f1=0.0504 (buggy), eval_loss=0.7545\\n\\n### 全量 dev eval\\nr1/r2a/r2b 全量 eval 并行运行中（cuda:2/1/3），预计 ~1h 后出结果。\\n真实数字出来后再做 ablation 结论。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## RSFT 文献对标 (2026-05-15)\\n\\n### 直接竞争者：无\\n在 DocRED 上没有 rejection sampling / self-training + NLI-based reward 的已发表工作。\\n\\n### 我们的定位\\n- +5.98pp rel_f1 显著超过同类 LLM-based DocRE 改进（RelPrior 2025: +2.06-2.38pp）\\n- CED 作为 rejection sampling 打分函数是新颖的方法论贡献\\n- 最接近的工作是 Progressive Self-distillation (ACM 2024) 用 knowledge distillation，但无 evidence-based reward\\n\\n---\\n\\n## CED-as-reranker 探索 (2026-05-15)\\n\\nRSFT 成功后仍保留 CED-as-reranker PoC 作为推理时替代方案（无需额外训练）。\\ncuda:0 上 PID 178050 运行中（985 docs × N=8，预计 ~6h）。\\n如果 reranker 接近或超过 RSFT，可作为论文中的 complementary contribution。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Qwen3 generation_config 覆盖陷阱 (2026-05-15)\\n\\nQwen3 模型自带 `generation_config.json` 会静默覆盖代码传参：\\n- `model.generation_config.top_k = 20`（限制采样池）\\n- `model.generation_config.temperature = 0.6`（覆盖代码传的任何温度值）\\n\\n**必须在生成前显式覆盖**：\\n```python\\nmodel.generation_config.temperature = target_temp\\nmodel.generation_config.top_k = 0  # 禁用 top_k 限制\\nmodel.generation_config.do_sample = True\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Diversity 监控计算 bug (2026-05-15)\\n\\n### 根因\\n- triple-set 归一化逻辑不一致\\n- diversity 分母用配置值而非实际生成数\\n\\n### 修复\\n- 提取 `diversity_metrics.py` 独立模块\\n- 修复后真实数据：302 docs mean_diversity=7.52/8, median=8.0/8\\n\\n---\\n\\n## RSFT 代码参数速查\\n\\n**rsft_generate.py**: --base_model, --sft_adapter, --data_path, --split, --output_dir, --num_generations(8), --temperature(0.7), --batch_size(1), --max_new_tokens(1024), --seed, --quantize/--no-quantize, --start_idx, --end_idx, --shard_id\\n\\n**rsft_score_filter.py**: --input_dir, --output_path, --nli_model_path, --nli_device, --tau(0.5), --f1_threshold(0.1), --selection_strategy(top_k), --top_k(2)\\n\\n**rsft_trainer.py**: --base_model, --sft_adapter, --rsft_data_path, --output_dir, --learning_rate, --num_epochs, --per_device_batch_size, --gradient_accumulation_steps, --lora_rank(64), --lora_alpha(128), --weighted_sampling, --quantize(False), complementary contribution。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Qwen3 generation_config 覆盖陷阱 (2026-05-15)\\n\\nQwen3 模型自带 `generation_config.json` 会静默覆盖代码传参：\\n- `model.generation_config.top_k = 20`（限制采样池）\\n- `model.generation_config.temperature = 0.6`（覆盖代码传的任何温度值）\\n\\n**必须在生成前显式覆盖**：\\n```python\\nmodel.generation_config.temperature = target_temp\\nmodel.generation_config.top_k = 0  # 禁用 top_k 限制\\nmodel.generation_config.do_sample = True\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Diversity 监控计算 bug (2026-05-15)\\n\\n### 根因\\n- triple-set 归一化逻辑不一致\\n- diversity 分母用配置值而非实际生成数\\n\\n### 修复\\n- 提取 `diversity_metrics.py` 独立模块\\n- 修复后真实数据：302 docs mean_diversity=7.52/8, median=8.0/8\\n\\n---\\n\\n## RSFT 代码参数速查\\n\\n**rsft_generate.py**: --base_model, --sft_adapter, --data_path, --split, --output_dir, --num_generations(8), --temperature(0.7), --batch_size(1), --max_new_tokens(1024), --seed, --quantize/--no-quantize, --start_idx, --end_idx, --shard_id\\n\\n**rsft_score_filter.py**: --input_dir, --output_path, --nli_model_path, --nli_device, --tau(0.5), --f1_threshold(0.1), --selection_strategy(top_k), --top_k(2)\\n\\n**rsft_trainer.py**: --base_model, --sft_adapter, --rsft_data_path, --output_dir, --learning_rate, --num_epochs, --per_device_batch_size, --gradient_accumulation_steps, --lora_rank(64), --lora_alpha(128), --weighted_sampling, --quantize(False), --eval_data_dir, --eval_max_docs, --wandb_run_name, --seed\", \"total_chars\": 4870, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have all the ablation data. Let me compile the full comparison:

**r2a results** (1 epoch, alpha=128/r=64 scaling=2.0, lr=2e-5, clean 2548 data):
- rel_f1 = 0.4494
- evi_f1 = 0.8108
- EDCR = 0.6641
- pred_count = 15482
- format_ok = 985/985 (100%)
- field_compliance = 778/985 (79.0%)
- truncated = 208/985 (21.1%)


- inference_time = 5967.2s

Generating comparison table...

Looking at the full results, all four configurations exceed the baseline performance with rel_f1 scores above 0.4057 and evi_f1 scores above 0.7979, so they all pass the MVP-2 threshold.

The rel_f1 scores cluster tightly around 0.449-0.450 for most runs, with r1 s44 standing out at 0.4655. Comparing r2a and r2b directly shows almost no difference despite their different hyperparameters, and they're essentially equivalent to r1 s42 as well.

The ablation results suggest that epoch count and data cleaning have minimal impact on performance—the small differences between single-epoch clean runs and three-epoch dirty runs are within noise margins. This indicates the original hypothesis about overfitting and truncated data causing poor performance was likely an artifact of the buggy evaluation, and the actual model performance was solid from the start.

LoRA scaling also makes almost no difference: comparing 2.0 versus 0.5 scaling yields negligible changes in both rel_f1 and evi_f1. However, r2b's more conservative scaling does produce slightly tighter outputs with better truncation rates and field compliance.

The EDCR metric reveals a consistent problem across all configurations—roughly two-thirds of evidence citations point to distractor sentences, suggesting this is a fundamental issue with how the model selects evidence rather than something configuration-dependent. Seed variance appears to be the dominant factor driving performance differences, overshadowing any effects from hyperparameter choices.

I'm noticing some GPU utilization issues: r2a is still showing as running while cuda:1 sits idle, and now both cuda:1 and cuda:3 are inactive. The ablation itself turned out to be inconclusive since all configurations perform similarly, which means the original hypothesis about epoch count, data quality, or LoRA scaling being root causes doesn't hold up. r2a eval has also completed. Let me compile all the results:

**r2a (1 epoch, α=128, lr=2e-5, clean 2548 data)**:
- rel_f1 = 0.4494
- ign_f1 = 0.4494
- evi_f1 = 0.8108
- EDCR = 0.6641
- precision = 0.4512, recall = 0.4475
- pred_count = 15482 (unique triples 12052?)
- format_ok = 985/985 (100%)


- field_compliance = 778/985 (79.0%)
- truncated = 208/985
- inference_time = 5967.2s

Now I'm building out the full comparison table across all configurations to see how r2a stacks up against r1 and the other variants. Looking at the results, all configurations exceed the MVP thresholds, but r2a and r2b perform nearly identically despite different hyperparameters, suggesting those changes had minimal impact. Comparing r1 s42 to r2a shows that switching to one epoch with cleaned data yields only marginal gains—a slight rel_f1 improvement offset by a small evi_f1 decline.

The standout is r1 s44, which achieves the highest rel_f1 at 0.4655, possibly from seed variance or the benefit of sticking with three epochs. Looking at secondary metrics, the conservative LoRA scaling in r2b improves field compliance and reduces truncations slightly, while r1 s44 shows the lowest distractor citation rate, suggesting it's generating more focused evidence selections.

The ablation itself is inconclusive—r2a and r2b perform nearly identically to r1 s42, with only r1 s44 breaking out. This actually demonstrates that RSFT is fairly robust across these hyperparameter choices. The real issue is that r2a conflates two changes at once (epoch count and data source), so I can't cleanly separate their individual effects. To resolve this, I'd need either an r1 config trained on clean data or an r2a variant using the original dataset instead.

I'm also noticing that cuda:1 and cuda:3 are sitting idle right now while s43_r2 runs on cuda:2 and the CED reranker occupies cuda:0—only half the GPU capacity is in use. The director should be aware of this for planning the next round of experiments. Now I'm ready to compile the full audit report for r2a and r2b.

r2a eval is also done. Let me compile the full ablation comparison and post an audit report. I now have all the data:

**r2a results** (1 epoch, α=128, lr=2e-5, clean 2548 data):
- rel_f1 = 0.4494
- evi_f1 = 0.8108
- EDCR = 0.6641
- precision = 0.4512, recall = 0.4475
- pred_count = 15482 (via n_predictions)


- format_ok = 985/985 (100%)
- field_compliance = 778/985 (79.0%)
- truncated = 208/985
- inference_time = 5967.2s

Now I'm building the full comparison table across all configurations to see how r2a stacks up against r1 s42 and the other variants. Now I'm examining the key ablation insights from these runs. One epoch with clean data performs nearly as well as three epochs without it, suggesting the extra training isn't necessary. When I compare the conservative LoRA scaling with lower learning rate against the more aggressive setup, I get equivalent relevance scores but better field compliance and fewer truncations, which points to a more efficient configuration.

Looking across all the variants, the performance variance is quite tight and matches what I'd expect from cross-seed differences alone. This suggests RSFT itself is the main driver of improvement over baseline SFT—roughly 4-6 percentage points—rather than any specific hyperparameter choice. The ablations are somewhat confounded since they bundle multiple changes together, but the consistency across configs indicates the approach is fairly robust.

I should also note that a couple of GPUs are now sitting idle, and I need to update the registry status since r2a has actually finished despite still showing as running, while r2b is properly marked as done.

Let me pull together the full ablation audit report now that we have both r2a and r2b results.

  "text": "## 审查报告 — RSFT r2a/r2b Ablation Eval 综合审查\n\n**结论**: PASS（附 2 个 warning）\n\n### 核实结果（metrics.json 独立验证，与 registry 一致）\n\n| Config | rel_f1 | evi_f1 | EDCR | 截断 | 字段完整率 | 预测数 |\n|--------|--------|--------|------|------|-----------|--------|\n| r1 s42 (3ep, α=128, lr=2e-5, 2718) | 0.4449 | 0.8187 | 0.6565 | 213 | 78.2% | 15307 |\n| r1 s44 (同上) | 0.4655 | 0.8087 | 0.6350 | — | — | — |\n| **r2a** (1ep, α=128, lr=2e-5, 2548 clean) | **0.4494** | **0.8108** | 0.6641 | 208 | 79.0% | 15482 |\n| **r2b** (1ep, α=32, lr=1e-5, 2548 clean) | **0.4493** | **0.8128** | 0.6609 | 179 | 81.8% | 14829 |\n| SFT baseline | 0.4057 | 0.7979 | — | — | — | — |\n\n**全部配置均通过 MVP-2 判据。**\n\n### Ablation 分析\n\n- [severity: info] **r2a vs r1 s42（隔离：1 epoch + clean data vs 3 epoch + 原始数据）**：rel_f1 +0.45pp（噪声级），evi_f1 -0.79pp。结论：3 epoch 无显著收益，1 epoch 足够。但注意这同时改了 epoch 数和数据（去除 170 个截断 JSON），两个变量混淆。\n\n- [severity: info] **r2b vs r2a（隔离：LoRA scaling + lr）**：rel_f1 几乎相同（Δ<0.01pp），evi_f1 +0.20pp，字段完整率更高（81.8% vs 79.0%），截断更少（179 vs 208）。保守 LoRA scaling (0.5) + 低 lr 在不损失精度的前提下略改善输出质量。\n\n- [severity: warning] **Ablation 变量混淆未解决**：r2a 改了 2 个变量（epoch + data），r2b 改了 4 个变量（epoch + data + LoRA alpha + lr）。所有配置结果在 ~0.45pp 范围内，差异 < 跨种子方差（s42 vs s44 Δrel_f1=2.06pp）。**无法归因任何单一因素的贡献**。如果论文需要 clean ablation story，建议追加控制实验（如：r1 config + clean data only，isolating data quality）。\n\n- [severity: warning] **EDCR 一致偏高（63.5%-66.4%）**：所有 RSFT 配置的 EDCR（证据中干扰句引用比例）均在 63-66% 范围。超过六成的证据引用是干扰句，说明 CED reward 在训练阶段改善了关系抽取但对证据精确度的提升有限。这与 evi_f1 提升仅 1-2pp（vs rel_f1 提升 4-6pp）一致。论文应坦诚讨论此局限性。\n\n- [severity: info] **RSFT 对超参鲁棒**：所有配置的 rel_f1 集中在 0.4449-0.4494（排除 s44 的 0.4655 高点），表明 RSFT+CED 方法本身是主要贡献（+4-6pp over SFT），具体超参选择影响很小。这是正面信号——方法鲁棒性好。\n\n### GPU 状态\n\n- cuda:0: CED reranker POC 运行中（54%）\n- cuda:1: **空闲**（r2a eval 完成）\n- cuda:2: s43_r2 eval 运行中（72%）\n- cuda:3: **空闲**（r2b eval 完成）\n\n两张卡空闲，建议安排后续任务。",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie"
}

_source line 594 · 2026-05-15T11:52:43.699Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
The log shows it started successfully. The inference script auto-detected the base model from the adapter config: "Detected adapter at /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_r2b, base: /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B". So --base_model and --sft_adapter were indeed optional.

However, I notice something important: the log says "Loading LoRA adapter: /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_r2b" but does NOT mention "Loading & merging SFT adapter". In the successful r2b eval, the log had:


```
Loading & merging SFT adapter: /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output
Loading LoRA adapter: /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_r2b
```

This means the maxtok1536 eval is missing the SFT adapter entirely—it's only applying the RSFT LoRA on top of the base model instead of the proper stack of Base → SFT → RSFT LoRA. That's a critical issue that would produce completely wrong results, so I need to flag this immediately.

Looking back at the logs, the original r2b command explicitly passed `--sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output`, which loaded and merged the SFT adapter first. The maxtok1536 command omitted that flag entirely, so the SFT adapter never gets loaded. This means the two evals are running fundamentally different model stacks, making the EDCR comparison invalid.

I need to stop this before it burns 100 minutes of GPU time on a flawed experiment.
````

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Good, let me analyze the ROADMAP update:

**Key changes in ROADMAP:**
1. Phase changed from MVP to "MVP 通过 → Research 启动中"
2. D039 declares all RSFT+CED ablation configs PASS MVP-2
3. GPU allocation updated to reflect current state
4. Post-MVP tasks listed: update paper_framing.md, create experiment_plan.yaml, dream_simulate, etc.
5. Research evaluation notes listed 6 items
6. GPU budget estimated: ~200 GPU-hours for Research phase

**My review needs to cover:**


The directional hypothesis holds up with 4 passing RSFT+CED configs across multiple seeds, but the ablation story has some gaps—I'm missing an isolated data cleaning ablation and haven't verified the SFT baseline EDCR comparison (that 0.71 number), plus s43 eval is still pending. The GRPO→RSFT pivot is well-supported by the data though.

GPU budget looks fine with 4× RTX 5090s providing roughly 128 GPU-hours daily, and the research needs about 200 GPU-hours total, so that's 1.5-2 days of full utilization without any time constraints. The paper narrative works—RSFT+CED as a pivot from GRPO is solid, EDCR as a metric contribution is interesting but really needs that SFT baseline comparison to land, and the GRPO catastrophic forgetting finding is well-evidenced. I'm noticing the notes frame CED's role as a filter narrative rather than a reward mechanism, which is a meaningful reframing.

The core issue is that EDCR actually gets worse under RSFT (0.66 versus the alleged 0.71 baseline), so presenting it as a contribution requires honest framing—claiming improvement on a metric where the method underperforms is risky. The post-MVP research phase plan exists but needs more concrete detail on what experiments will actually happen. Most critically, I need to examine whether the CED reframing from reward to evidence quality discriminator is a genuine insight or just rationalizing the GRPO failure.

I'm also noticing some experimental gaps: the cuda:3 ablation on CED scoring strictness is sensible, but the plan to expand baselines to 6+ doesn't specify which ones yet. Let me pull up the paper framing document to see how the narrative currently handles these tensions before I write my review.
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — FREIGE\\n\\n> **状态**: POST-MVP (MVP-1 + MVP-2 均 PASS，进入 Research 阶段)\\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-15\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Stretch: ARR May 25 (10 days, 极紧，仅在实验全部顺利时考虑)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nLLM-based document-level IE 已逼近 encoder SOTA 的抽取精度，但零方法能生成可验证证据。朴素 NLI faithfulness reward 可被 entity-mentioning distractor 句子游戏——共享实体但不支持具体关系的句子仍获正面蕴含分数。\\n\\n**CED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination)** 通过文档内 hard negative 对比，将 evidence 质量评估从\\\"是否蕴含\\\"升级为\\\"是否比最佳干扰句更蕴含\\\"——本质上是 evidence grounding 场景下的 **anti-reward-overoptimization mechanism**（连接 Gao et al. 2023 reward overoptimization 理论）。\\n\\n**MVP 关键发现**: CED 作为在线 RL (GRPO) reward 导致 catastrophic forgetting（3 种 reward type × 多个 lr 全线失败），但作为离线 RL (RSFT) 的候选筛选信号极其有效——4 组超参配置均稳定超过 SFT baseline。**方法论结论**: 对结构化 IE 任务，CED 应作为离线质量信号而非在线训练奖励。\\n\\n## Story Arc\\n\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍（human-in-the-loop KB population、生物医学审计、RAG 溯源）\\n2. **Why naive reward fails**: flat-NLI 被 entity-sharing distractors 欺骗（SFT EDCR=71.2%，大量引用干扰句）\\n3. **Why online RL fails**: GRPO+CED/flat-NLI/extract-only 全部 catastrophic forgetting（reward ↑ + rel_f1 ↓ 至 <0.02）\\n4. **Solution**: CED 作为离线质量信号 + RSFT（rejection sampling fine-tuning）：SFT 模型生成 N 候选 → CED 打分筛选 → 微调，规避在线 RL 的遗忘风险\\n5. **Theoretical grounding**: 连接 LongRLVR (ICLR 2026) evidence grounding 梯度消失分析 + \\\"RSFT=PPO\\\" (ICML 2025) 等价性理论\\n6. **Result**: 首个 LLM-based DocRE 方法同时生成抽取和可验证证据\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Main): CED-scored RSFT 使 LLM 同时生成抽取和忠实证据\\n- FREIGE 是首个在 DocRED 上同时输出 (head, relation, tail, [evidence]) 的 LLM 方法\\n- RSFT+CED: rel_f1 +3.9~6.0pp over SFT, evi_f1 +1.1~2.1pp, EDCR -4.8~7.7pp\\n- **MVP-2 已验证**: 4/4 configs PASS\\n- **Research 需要**: test set 评估，DREEAM/SAIS Evi-F1 直接对比\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Ablation): CED scoring 优于 flat-NLI scoring 用于 RSFT\\n- CED-filtered RSFT vs flat-NLI-filtered RSFT 的 evi_f1 和 EDCR 差异\\n- CED 的对比性 margin 过滤掉 distractor-heavy 候选\\n- **Research 需要**: flat-NLI scored RSFT ablation（核心实验）\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Analysis): 在线 RL 在结构化 IE 上 catastrophically forget\\n- GRPO+QLoRA 在 3 种 reward type × 多个 lr 下全线失败\\n- 关键对比: evi_f1 在 GRPO 下提升至 0.850（>SFT 0.798）但 rel_f1 崩至 <0.02——CED 驱动了 evidence 学习但在线 RL 摧毁了抽取能力\\n- RSFT 规避此问题：不修改 policy gradient，只筛选训练数据\\n- **MVP 已有完整证据**: D031/D033 GRPO 判死，D039 RSFT 成功\\n\\n### Claim 4 (Evaluation): EDCR 作为独立评估指标\\n- EDCR = |entity-sharing non-gold cited sentences| / |total cited sentences|\\n- Evi-F1 盲区：不惩罚 plausible-looking distractor citations\\n- SFT EDCR=71.2%, RSFT+CED EDCR=63.5~66.4%（方向正确但差距有限，需坦诚讨论）\\n- **Research 需要**: cross-method diagnostic table, CED vs flat-NLI EDCR 对比\\n\\n## MVP Evidence Summary\\n\\n| Config | rel_f1 | Δ rel_f1 | evi_f1 | EDCR | 状态 |\\n|--------|--------|----------|--------|------|------|\\n| SFT baseline | 0.4057 | — | 0.7979 | 0.7118 | ✅ |\\n| RSFT r1 (3ep, α=128, s42) | 0.4449 | +3.92pp | 0.8187 | 0.6565 | ✅ |\\n| RSFT r1 (s44) | 0.4655 | +5.98pp | 0.8087 | 0.6350 | ✅ |\\n| RSFT r2a (1ep, α=128) | 0.4494 | +4.37pp | 0.8108 | 0.6641 | ✅ |\\n| RSFT r2b (1ep, α=32, lr=1e-5) | 0.4493 | +4.36pp | 0.8128 | 0.6609 | ✅ |\\n| GRPO best (lr=5e-5, ckpt-90) | 0.2110 | -19.5pp | 0.5750 | — | ❌ |\\n| GRPO best (lr=5e-6, ckpt-100) | 0.0138 | -39.2pp | 0.8500 | 0.9900 | ❌ |\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### RC1: NLI reward 信号质量（高概率攻击）\\n- **攻击**: \\\"DeBERTa-v3-base 的 NLI 判断有 lexical overlap bias，CED 训练信号有噪声\\\"\\n- **已验证**: MVP-1 Pearson r=0.34（NLI vs lexical overlap），远低于 0.7，证明非捷径\\n- **Research 补充**: DeBERTa-base vs DeBERTa-large 对比\\n\\n### RC2: Gold evidence 标注不完整导致 false distractor\\n- **攻击**: \\\"被标为 distractor 的句子可能是 annotator 遗漏的有效证据\\\"\\n- **应对**: CED margin 的 self-correcting property——如果 false distractor 真正蕴含关系，其 p_neg 高 → margin 小 → 惩罚弱\\n- **Research 补充**: false-distractor 鲁棒性量化分析\\n\\n### RC3: 评测可比性\\n- **攻击**: \\\"生成式 evidence 如何与 DREEAM 的 attention-based evidence 做 apple-to-apple 对比？\\\"\\n- **应对**: 模型输出显式句子索引 [S_i]，直接映射句子级 Evi-F1；辅助指标用 RoBERTa-large-MNLI（非训练用 DeBERTa）避免 circular evaluation\\n\\n### RC4: 单数据集 + 单模型泛化性\\n- **攻击**: \\\"只在 DocRED + Qwen3-4B 上验证\\\"\\n- **Research**: Re-DocRED 扩展 + Qwen3-1.7B/8B model size ablation\\n\\n### RC5: RSFT vs GRPO 是否公平对比\\n- **攻击**: \\\"GRPO 失败可能是超参问题而非方法论问题\\\"\\n- **应对**: 完整 GRPO 轨迹——3 种 reward × 多个 lr × 全 checkpoint eval；D020 控制变量下 CED 是唯一存活的 reward type\\n\\n### RC6: EDCR 63-66% 仍然偏高，CED 对证据精确度提升有限\\n- **攻击**: \\\"RSFT+CED 的 EDCR 只比 SFT 低 5-8pp，证据质量提升不够显著\\\"\\n- **应对**: (1) CED 在 RSFT 中是间接作用（筛选训练数据），非直接 reward；(2) CED-as-reranker 可能更直接降低 EDCR；(3) 需要 flat-NLI RSFT 对比证明 CED 方向正确\\n- **Research**: CED reranker PoC（运行中），top_k ablation\\n\\n### RC7: 方法过于简单\\n- **应对**: 简洁是优点——RSFT 方法论已被证明理论等价于 PPO (ICML 2025)，CED 创新在于打分函数设计而非框架复杂度\\n\\n## Key Baselines (8+)\\n\\n1. **SFT-only**: Qwen3-4B + LoRA SFT on DocRED gold — 无 RL\\n2. **RSFT flat-NLI**: flat-NLI scored RSFT — 无 contrastive signal（核心 ablation）\\n3. **RSFT CED (FREIGE)**: CED scored RSFT — 主方法\\n4. **CED-as-reranker**: 推理时 CED 重排 SFT 候选（无训练）— 运行中\\n5. **GRPO+CED**: 在线 RL with CED reward — negative baseline\\n6. **DREEAM**: RoBERTa-large encoder — evidence SOTA (Evi-F1 57.34)\\n7. **SAIS**: RoBERTa encoder — evidence baseline (Evi-F1 55.67)\\n8. **DocKS-RAG**: GNN+RAG — extraction SOTA (F1 63.59, 无 evidence)\\n9. **LMRC**: LLaMA3.1-8B — generative LLM SOTA (F1 60.69, 无 evidence)\\n\\n## Publishability Threshold\\n\\n- **必要条件**: RSFT+CED 在 test set 上 Evi-F1 > DREEAM 57.34 AND rel_f1 显著 > SFT baseline\\n- **充分条件**: 上述 + CED vs flat-NLI RSFT Evi-F1/EDCR 差异显著 + GRPO 分析完整\\n- **Failure pivot**: Evi-F1 < 57.34 on test set → 转为 analysis contribution（在线 vs 离线 RL for structured IE）\\n\\n## Research Phase Priorities\\n\\n| Priority | 实验 | Claim | 预估 GPU-hours |\\n|----------|------|-------|---------------|\\n| P0 | CED vs flat-NLI RSFT ablation | C2 | ~20h |\\n| P0 | Test set evaluation (RSFT best config) | C1 | ~4h |\\n| P0 | EDCR pipeline 验证 + cross-method table | C4 | ~2h |\\n| P0 | DREEAM/SAIS Evi-F1 baseline reproduction | C1 | ~8h |\\n| P1 | CED-as-reranker (running) | C1 bonus | ~6h |\\n| P1 | Model size ablation (1.7B/8B) | RC4 | ~24h |\\n| P1 | Re-DocRED extension | RC4 | ~12h |\\n| P1 | top_k ablation (k=1 vs k=3) | RC6 | ~8h |\\n| P2 | GRPO forgetting 详细分析 + 可视化 | C3 | ~4h |\\n| P2 | DeBERTa-base vs large NLI 对比 | RC1 | ~4h |\\n\", \"total_chars\": 6099, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

FREIGE\\n\\n> **状态**: POST-MVP (MVP-1 + MVP-2 均 PASS，进入 Research 阶段)\\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-15\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Stretch: ARR May 25 (10 days, 极紧，仅在实验全部顺利时考虑)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nLLM-based document-level IE 已逼近 encoder SOTA 的抽取精度，但零方法能生成可验证证据。朴素 NLI faithfulness reward 可被 entity-mentioning distractor 句子游戏——共享实体但不支持具体关系的句子仍获正面蕴含分数。\\n\\n**CED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination)** 通过文档内 hard negative 对比，将 evidence 质量评估从\\\"是否蕴含\\\"升级为\\\"是否比最佳干扰句更蕴含\\\"——本质上是 evidence grounding 场景下的 **anti-reward-overoptimization mechanism**（连接 Gao et al. 2023 reward overoptimization 理论）。\\n\\n**MVP 关键发现**: CED 作为在线 RL (GRPO) reward 导致 catastrophic forgetting（3 种 reward type × 多个 lr 全线失败），但作为离线 RL (RSFT) 的候选筛选信号极其有效——4 组超参配置均稳定超过 SFT baseline。**方法论结论**: 对结构化 IE 任务，CED 应作为离线质量信号而非在线训练奖励。\\n\\n## Story Arc\\n\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍（human-in-the-loop KB population、生物医学审计、RAG 溯源）\\n2. **Why naive reward fails**: flat-NLI 被 entity-sharing distractors 欺骗（SFT EDCR=71.2%，大量引用干扰句）\\n3. **Why online RL fails**: GRPO+CED/flat-NLI/extract-only 全部 catastrophic forgetting（reward ↑ + rel_f1 ↓ 至 <0.02）\\n4. **Solution**: CED 作为离线质量信号 + RSFT（rejection sampling fine-tuning）：SFT 模型生成 N 候选 → CED 打分筛选 → 微调，规避在线 RL 的遗忘风险\\n5. **Theoretical grounding**: 连接 LongRLVR (ICLR 2026) evidence grounding 梯度消失分析 + \\\"RSFT=PPO\\\" (ICML 2025) 等价性理论\\n6. **Result**: 首个 LLM-based DocRE 方法同时生成抽取和可验证证据\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Main): CED-scored RSFT 使 LLM 同时生成抽取和忠实证据\\n- FREIGE 是首个在 DocRED 上同时输出 (head, relation, tail, [evidence]) 的 LLM 方法\\n- RSFT+CED: rel_f1 +3.9~6.0pp over SFT, evi_f1 +1.1~2.1pp, EDCR -4.8~7.7pp\\n- **MVP-2 已验证**: 4/4 configs PASS\\n- **Research 需要**: test set 评估，DREEAM/SAIS Evi-F1 直接对比\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Ablation): CED scoring 优于 flat-NLI scoring 用于 RSFT\\n- CED-filtered RSFT vs flat-NLI-filtered RSFT 的 evi_f1 和 EDCR 差异\\n- CED 的对比性 margin 过滤掉 distractor-heavy 候选\\n- **Research 需要**: flat-NLI scored RSFT ablation（核心实验）\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Analysis): 在线 RL 在结构化 IE 上 catastrophically forget\\n- GRPO+QLoRA 在 3 种 reward type × 多个 lr 下全线失败\\n- 关键对比: evi_f1 在 GRPO 下提升至 0.850（>SFT 0.798）但 rel_f1 崩至 <0.02——CED 驱动了 evidence 学习但在线 RL 摧毁了抽取能力\\n- RSFT 规避此问题：不修改 policy gradient，只筛选训练数据\\n- **MVP 已有完整证据**: D031/D033 GRPO 判死，D039 RSFT 成功\\n\\n### Claim 4 (Evaluation): EDCR 作为独立评估指标\\n- EDCR = |entity-sharing non-gold cited sentences| / |total cited sentences|\\n- Evi-F1 盲区：不惩罚 plausible-looking distractor citations\\n- SFT EDCR=71.2%, RSFT+CED EDCR=63.5~66.4%（方向正确但差距有限，需坦诚讨论）\\n- **Research 需要**: cross-method diagnostic table, CED vs flat-NLI EDCR 对比\\n\\n## This is a major update. Let me do a thorough ROADMAP review.

**Positive aspects:**
1. MVP-2 conclusions are well-supported by data (I've verified all the numbers independently)
2. The pivot from GRPO to RSFT is data-driven (D031-D033 evidence chain)
3. Paper framing is honest about GRPO failure — turning it into Claim 3 (analysis contribution)
4. Anticipated reviewer concerns are comprehensive and realistic
5. Baseline list is strong (8+ baselines)
6. Research priorities are well-structured with GPU-hour estimates


7. The EDCR limitation is acknowledged and we're being transparent about the scope

**Concerns to flag:**

Now I need to verify some critical numbers. The SFT baseline EDCR of 0.7118 is foundational to our RSFT improvement claims, but I haven't traced where this came from in the audited evaluations — I should check the registry entries. Similarly, I need to confirm the GRPO evidence metrics that are cited.

The EDCR improvement range of -4.8 to 7.7pp is directionally correct but modest, which the paper already acknowledges. For the publishability threshold, we're claiming RSFT+CED test set evidence F1 should exceed DREEAM's 57.34, which is an ambitious target.

However, there's a critical issue here — the dev set evidence F1 is 0.808-0.819, which seems much higher than 57.34, but these might be on different evaluation protocols entirely. The paper itself flags this as a reviewer concern: comparing generative evidence against DREEAM's attention-based approach isn't straightforward without ensuring we're measuring the same thing. This comparison could be misleading if the metrics aren't aligned.

The experiment plan YAML is still missing, which blocks a proper coverage audit. On the positive side, the CED reframing from reward mechanism to filter is well-supported by the empirical evidence — GRPO failed while RSFT succeeded — so this isn't just post-hoc rationalization.

For timeline and resources, the May 25 deadline is realistic as a stretch goal, with July as the primary target. At 200 GPU-hours and 4 RTX 5090s available, we're looking at roughly 1.5-2 days of full utilization, which fits comfortably within the 2-month window. GPU budget isn't a constraint here.

The ROADMAP results show r2a/r2b metrics but don't address the confounding variables I flagged earlier—that's going to be a vulnerability if it makes it into the paper. I should verify the RSFT=PPO theoretical grounding from Tang et al. 2024 and check whether there's a 2025 formalization, since that's a key reference point for the alignment approach.
